To sum up what was obtained in this secret room:

“A mysterious golden book with the story of a brother and sister written inside.”

Well, I still couldn’t tell its immediate use.

But the rest of the loot(?) was a different story.

“Seven summoning books.”

Not just any summoning books, but each one was a book that couldn’t be found on any outside bookshelf.

Like one containing more than ten rare mimics.

Strangely, the monster illustrations weren’t black and white but painted over in colors like blue and red.

Or parts of the monster images were marked with ‘???’.

Seven books with such peculiarities.

“A suspicious fountain pen.”

On top of that, there was the fountain pen that had been on Hamshik’s desk.

“Hamshik, how do you use this fountain pen?”

[W-well...]

After hesitating for a long time, Hamshik finally started honestly, saying, “Well, since you’re a friend...”

[If you use this pen to paint over the drawings in the book... you can obtain the essence of that color.]

“What?!”

[Do-don’t shout! You startled me!]

No, then say it cautiously so I’m not surprised.

A fountain pen that guarantees you get the essence of the desired color...?

Is it even right for such an item to exist in this world?

It felt like an absurdly broken item, but unfortunately, the fountain pen had limits.

Grade 3 or below only.

And it could only be used twice.

“Well, there’s no way it would be unlimited use.”

Disappointment came over me, but since the opponent was [Dungeon and Stone], it felt a bit less bad.

It was just that I expected the item to be a bit foolish...

For this game, this kind of item was already insanely strong.

“Is this an unexplored region...?”

I could understand why explorers were so obsessed with the newly opened underground first floor.

The 5th floor, 6th floor, 7th floor too.

There must have been such broken hidden pieces when they were first opened.

“By the way, Hamshik, how did you know about the fountain pen? Did you try using it a few times before I arrived?”

[No way!]

“Hmm, but your explanation was surprisingly detailed.”

[I just knew! Since I was born!]

Hamshik said he never used it but knew what the fountain pen was for from the start. fɾeewebnovel.co๓

[Why would you say that!]

As I naturally tucked the pen in my pocket, Hamshik had another fit.

[Th-that fountain pen is something that must be handed to a qualified person...!]

Oh, really?

“But you said it can be used twice, right?”

[Yes, but...]

“Well, you can use it once, then give it to the qualified person later.”

[W-what nonsense—!]

“Or is there some rule that it must be given in a perfect condition?”

[U-uh... not exactly...]

“Then there’s no problem!”

Okay, so the fountain pen issue ends here.

I’ll go out, use it once, then after thinking more about where to use the remaining use, persuade the qualified person.

So, with that in mind, I turned my attention to the last loot.

“A map...”

It looked like a condensed map of the entire underground first floor.

Centered on the starting point, Rock Island, several islands were drawn...

Though the fountain pen was a great item, this map was also a huge gain.

Because the time needed to explore and light up the map was reduced.

“There are still two islands we haven’t visited.”

After consulting navigator Auyen, one was about a week’s distance from the library island.

The other was about a month away.

“A month... it’s a much larger floor than expected.”

“Yes. But there’s one concern... The location of this island is in the direction where that rainbow was seen.”

“So that means this island could be the one beyond the rainbow?”

“Well... I can’t say for sure yet, but I think it’s not just a coincidence.”

I thought so too.

For that reason, let’s call this island the Rainbow Island from now on...

“Hamshik, do you know what’s beyond this map?”

[I don’t know...!]

Hamshik answered sulkily while looking around the mess of the room.

He seemed a little upset but didn’t seem to be lying.

‘So he really doesn’t ✧ NоvеIight ✧ (Original source) know...’

It’s better to assume this map doesn’t show everything.

My curiosity only grew.

What happens if we go beyond this map? Or is it even possible to leave?

Back when people believed the world was flat, I guess that’s why they were so curious about the edge of the world.

‘Anyway, I’ll find out when I go myself someday...’

“Yandel, look at this part here.”

Versil pointed to a spot on the unfolded map.

It was the part with Giant Island, specifically the cave where we stayed on the island.

“Do you see the symbol marked here?”

“This looks like the portal stele.”

“Exactly, right?”

The stele symbol drawn on the cave part.

But below that symbol was a very intuitive mark.

“An arrow pointing downward...”

What could that mean?

‘Underground second floor.’

Everyone looking at the map seemed to think the same thing, but no one spoke it aloud.

Then, when silence stretched out...

“...Shall we look here too?”

Versil moved her finger to point to a new location on the map.

Auyen was the first to identify it.

“This is... the Chief’s Island...”

“That’s right. There are only two stele marks on this entire map, and one of them is here.”

The moment Versil confirmed the spot she pointed at, a suffocating feeling hit me.

I didn’t know whether to take it as good news or bad news.

“At least we found the exit.”

The arrow below the stele symbol clearly pointed upward.

We found the exit.

No, to be exact, we discovered a place presumed to be the exit.

Just that was excellent news for us, who had been wandering the vast sea without any clues.

But...

‘Of all places, it’s the Chief’s Island...’

This fact took my breath away.

Because to return to the city, whether I liked it or not, I’d have to face the Chief someday.

“So, can we go back to the city now?”

“It’s doneee! Waaaaaah!!”

Some members cheered with joy just from finding the exit, but unfortunately, there were still several problems left to escape this place.

“But... is escaping really possible? The stele on Giant Island didn’t work either...”

“That’s true. Like when we arrived, there must be some condition we need to meet.”

As some intellectuals said, finding the exit isn’t the end. We must first find a way to activate the portal stele.

Getting past the Chief will be the next problem.

But...

“Rainbow! The island in the direction of that rainbow! There must be a key there!”

There were a few things worth trying. We hadn’t just come here to twiddle our thumbs.

‘The most likely candidate is Rainbow Island, but even if it’s not, if we conquer all the islands, we’ll naturally find out.’

In that sense, I asked Hamshik one more thing.

“Hamshik, what monsters appear on the top shelf?”

How much do we need to level up to clear this library island?

Hamshik’s answer was simple.

He gave examples of the explorers’ standards without clearly saying the exact grades...

[Tol-Lafupa, Kashan, Leviathan, Prahel, Ridger...]

Sigh, so in the end, we have to catch even a grade 1.

Still, it’s a relief that it’s only one, not many.

“What happens if we catch them?”

Hamshik spoke to me in a suddenly serious tone.

[I don’t know.]

“Don’t you know if you gain the ‘qualification’ you mentioned?”

[Again, I don’t know. I have no memory of that.]

Yeah, so no freebies here; we have to find out ourselves.

“Hamshik, how do we get out? Should we go through that portal-looking place?”

After finishing the investigation, we thought there was nothing more to gain here.

Hamshik tilted his head.

[Huh? Once you come in, you can’t get out.]

“...What? Why are you telling me that now—.”

[Beep! Just kidding! You can get out that way!]

Ah, I really got startled.

I gave a dumb smile, and Hamshik laughed happily at me.

[Beep-beep! Isn’t it common sense for friends to joke around!]

Seeing how happy he was, I couldn’t even get mad.

I chuckled and led the team outside.

The stone gate was still wide open and glowing, but as soon as Hamshik came out last, the light disappeared and the door closed.

“But Hamshik, do you have to open the door like that every time you go in?”

[Why?]

“I thought that’s where you live. Wouldn’t it be inconvenient to open it every time?”

[I don’t know? It wasn’t inconvenient at all normally! I just couldn’t get in because you guys kept coming while I was organizing books!]

Ah, so that’s why he couldn’t even get home and was wandering around before we caught him.

He must have been going out only at night because we were asleep then.

“After a nap, I’ll hunt again. What will you do in the meantime?”

[Watch!]

“Organizing books?”

[When no one’s around... when you guys leave and no one is around.]

“Why? Oh, I’m not telling you to do it now, just curious. Since you’ve been seen by us anyway, you could do it anytime, right?”

[...I’ll do it when no one’s around. So don’t force me.]

Well, I have nothing to say then.

After finishing the secret room exploration, we returned to the library and resumed the paused camping.

And as soon as morning came, we started hunting immediately.

「Mimic defeated.」

「Mimic defeated.」

「Mimic defeated.」

「Mimic defeated.」

「Mimi—」

First, I summoned mimics using the mimic summoning book obtained in the secret room. Unfortunately, even though I killed more than ten, no essences dropped.

Essences need to drop to loot items from the [Treasure Vault].

‘Not as disappointing as I thought.’

Maybe because I knew the best mimic drop would be about thirty million stones.

I didn’t really feel too bad.

It wasn’t the only summoning book I got this time.

So I opened all the other summoning books too.

“...Drake?”

From the book marked with ‘???’, a grade 4 flying monster, Drake, was summoned.

What? I was secretly nervous about what would come out.

Was it really 100% random?

I don’t know, but all it dropped was a single magic stone.

“So now, only the painted books remain.”

Five summoning books with colored monsters inside.

After opening them all, there was a common feature.

The book with the monster painted red dropped that monster’s red essence, and the one painted blue dropped blue essence.

‘The problem was that except for one grade 3 essence, they were all grade 5 or below.’

Essences below grade 5 were discarded without a second thought, and the grade 3 essence was handed over to the Armin expedition team who wanted it.

And...

‘All opened already.’

Thus, all the summoning books brought from the secret room were completely used up without much gain.

Well, the main dish—the fountain pen—remained...

‘It’s right to wait and consider carefully until the end before using it.’

I want to use it now to extract the essence of Bellarios, but I made the most rational decision.

Since I’m not just hunting for one day.

There was a chance to get essence naturally, so using it already felt like a waste.

Therefore...

「Stonewinter defeated.」

「Mecarote defeated. EXP +7」

「Frost Guardian defeated.」

「Vol-Herchan defeated.」

「Machine Giant defeated...」

「...」

Morning hunt.

Lunch hunt.

Evening hunt until bedtime.

How many days did I continue like this?

The 99th day since entering the labyrinth.

‘Tomorrow the labyrinth will open again in the city.’

The time when newbies would start flowing in was approaching.

“Deputy Commander, did you hear the news?”

“Ah, that...”

Arua Raven, deputy commander of the Royal Third Mage Battalion, smiled bitterly.

Underground first floor, Records Archive.

No matter where you go in the city, that’s the only topic lately.

Because a shocking rumor had recently started circulating.

Bjorn Yandel was still alive and well there.

And he had two single numbers in his hands.

That rumor, originating from evil spirits and not confirmed as true or false.

“Deputy Commander... do you think that rumor is true?”

“Whether the rumor is true or not doesn’t matter. We just need to do our job well. I’ll get up first.”

Raven dismissed the subordinate’s question, finished her meal, and returned to her office.

Whether the rumor was true or not.

It was meaningless to worry about that.

She probably wished the same thing either way.

She hoped the rumor was true.

Then she wouldn’t have to worry anxiously every day.

“Sigh... If it’s true, it’s really annoying.”

Underground first floor.

An unexplored region with nothing revealed.

What kind of mystery and unknown things exist there? And what great expedition is Bjorn Yandel conducting with his companions?

“Phew...”

She sighed deeply and lowered her head.

The uniform she had become so familiar with.

And the mage battalion badge pinned to her chest.

Today, this outfit felt unusually stifling and annoying.

“Well, whatever. I’ll just do my job...”

Repeating the words she just told her subordinate, she started work again.

And that evening.

“Deputy Commander! There’s a direct royal dispatch from the palace!”

She received the royal dispatch.

Though filled with elaborate wording and difficult language, she could summarize it in three lines:

After reading the entire document, Raven jumped up from her chair like a fish out of water.

And hurriedly began packing.

Day 103 since entering the labyrinth.

At last, the number of days inside had surpassed three digits.

It was about time to take a break from grinding in the library.

From now on, we had to head to Rock Island to welcome the newbies.

‘I hope some decent ones come in so we can aim for the higher-tier books.’

Even now, when opening summoning books, four or five grade 3 monsters would appear, and occasionally a grade 2 would be mixed in.

Well, if the combination was good, we might manage to kill one or two...

But smoothly grinding like a factory was now impossible.

I played the tank role in prolonged battles and needed long rests afterward to recover consumed resources.

‘Last time, we almost had casualties...’

Anyway, increasing manpower was not an option but a necessity...

As we packed up the camp and prepared to leave, I felt eyes watching me from a distance.

Turning my head toward that direction, I saw Hamshik staring intently at me from afar.

[You’re leaving...?]

As I approached, Hamshik spoke while looking sternly at my feet.

The first meeting was awkward, but eventually we became friends, and he kept me company during all the hunting in the library, so it seemed we’d grown attached.

“Ah, don’t worry. I’ll be back soon. Besides, I’m not leaving the golden book behind, am I?”

[But you’re taking my fountain pen...!]

“Come on, friends overlook small things like that, understand?”

[...]

“No, I’m telling you, I’ll be back soon.”

[Who’s waiting? Don’t come back ever! It’s much better without people like you! Quiet!]

Haha, there he goes again.

I ignored Hamshik’s harsh words as a true barbarian would—just letting them pass in one ear and out the other.

“Baron, the whole squad is ready!”

“Good. Got it.”

Though we finished preparations to leave the library, for some reason I couldn’t easily take the first step.

[...]

“Hey, Hamshik.”

[...]

“Make sure to organize the books while I’m gone. Even if you’re not hungry, eat the jerky I gave you on time. And if any weird guys show up, never show your face. Hide behind the bookshelf and wait until I come back—”

[I’m not a kid! Stop the nonsense and leave if you’re going to!]

Still, even saying that—

Sigh... why do I feel so worried?

Maybe the attachment grew more on my side.

“Well... I’m going, Hamshik...”

[Yeah...]

“I’ll be back soon.”

[...Yeah.]

Forcing my reluctant feet to move, I turned my back and passed between the squad who had been watching from afar.

Smoothly, the squad opened the way for me to lead.

Ascending the stairs first, the others followed behind in formation as we began moving.

Once outside the island, we boarded the summoned ship and arrived at Rock Island within a few hours.

“No signs that anyone’s been here before us.”

I had come early just in case, but indeed, no explorer had descended to the underground first floor before us.

Well, it was only the third day since the labyrinth opened.

Physically impossible to finish farming on the third floor and return down to the first floor in that time.

“What are you thinking?”

“Just worried if Hamshik’s doing well.”

“I wonder exactly how you look to him... I can’t seem to approach him well given how I appear...”

“How do you look?”

“...Like a wizard. Holding a skull staff, looking even more evil than a lich, making horrible noises.”

Ah, that’s right.

Back then, I just listened and let it pass, but now with more samples collected, I think about it differently.

‘An evil wizard holding a skull staff...’

Isn’t that an imaginary image made by combining a corpse collector and a ruin scholar?

Of course, I didn’t say that aloud.

“Baron, may I take the team to the beach?”

“The beach?”

“Well... there’s always stuff floating in the sea, right? I thought maybe we could collect some during the downtime...”

“Do as you please. There’s nothing else to do but wait for a while anyway.”

Some explorers tired of waiting took the lead, and one by one others joined to pick up scrap by the beach.

It seemed the armor of grade 3 material we found during sailing had a big influence. Since then, talk had been spreading among us.

If luck follows, we might hit the jackpot.

Maybe even pick up equipment used by ancient heroes.

‘Well, if luck is really good, that kind of jackpot might happen...’

Honestly, I was skeptical.

We’d been everywhere but the most valuable thing found was the grade 3 armor picked up recently.

‘When the underground first floor fully opens later, there might be people who specialize in picking up scrap.’

Hmm, I thought that was inefficient, but that kind of group would probably appear.

Rock Island itself had no monsters.

If we can just avoid the rainy season on time, grinding here could be a source of income.

‘Time really doesn’t pass...’

After chatting idly at the start point all day, boredom finally got the better of me and I went to the beach to pick up scrap with the team.

And how much time passed?

A day, two days, three days...

By the end of day 107, only a few hours remained before the first floor closed again.

‘...They can’t not come by now. Did the royal bastards do something? Blocking entry?’

Unease slowly began to rise.

After all the bait thrown, we were expecting skilled newbies this time.

‘If they haven’t come yet... something must be wrong up there.’

Tsk, does that mean we have to continue the conquest alone without newbies this time?

While thinking that, I kept staring at the useless clock.

Whoooosh-!

“Portal! The portal has opened!”

Dozens of portals opened, and newbies stepped in.

How to handle the newly arrived newbies.

I wasn’t particularly worried.

First, I planned to show them around the underground first floor, letting them realize it was no easy place.

Then, once they were fully aware, just sign them to slave contracts and that would be it.

But...

‘Huh, no wonder no one came until the last day.’

When I saw the newbies who came this round, I had to admit it immediately.

They were opponents for whom that method would never work.

“Reform formation!”

“Reform!”

The moment they landed on the underground first floor, the group solemnly and neatly formed a formation.

I sighed.

‘Worst case scenario.’

Too many priests for a typical clan.

A mage battalion uniformed all in the same clothes.

And a group of knights in polished plate armor.

Though the classes could be divided into three major groups, they shared one thing in common.

“Nia Lapdonia...!”

They bore the royal crest on their chests.

Once formation was complete, a man who appeared to be their commander approached me and saluted briskly.

“It is my sincere honor to meet you, Baron Yandel! I never expected to meet you so soon. I’m truly relieved you’re still alive!”

“Who are you...?”

“Ah, I haven’t introduced myself yet. I’m Jillen Evost of the First Royal Knights.”

“Your relation to Baron Evost?”

“He’s my older brother.”

Sigh, at least the commander wasn’t very high-ranking.

Not the baron himself, but his younger brother.

If things went well, I might be able to crush him with my baron’s rank...

“Oh, and I’m temporarily serving as deputy commander of this expedition.”

That hope shattered with that one sentence.

“Deputy commander...?”

“Yes. Could you please step aside? The second expedition will enter soon.”

Stunned, I stepped back.

Whoooosh-!

Once again dozens of portals opened in the sky, and dozens more explorers entered the underground first floor.

Twice, at intervals.

‘Sixty people entered three times...’

One hundred eighty people.

A force worthy of the name ‘expedition army’ rather than just ‘expedition team.’

However, the real problem was different.

“Nia Lapdonia! Reporting that all 180 explorers sent by royal order have safely entered the destination, Commander!”

The commander heard the report immediately upon entry.

“No threats detected nearby. The primary target of this mission, Baron Bjorn Yandel, has been found. Of the total 38, seven are from Clan Anabada, and the rest are presumed to be members of the Hext Clan and Armin expedition who entered during the first and second waves!”

“I see.”

The commander with ghostly pale skin and silver hair walked toward me.

A very familiar face.

Though about twenty years older than when I last saw him full of youth.

“I remember seeing you from afar at the baron’s investiture but never greeting you properly.”

Head of the First Royal Knights.

The royal executioner known as the Knight of Light...

“Nice to meet you, Baron Yandel. I am Jerome Saintred.”

The same guy I had various incidents with twenty years ago, when he operated in Noirark wearing an iron mask.

“Y-yeah... nice to meet you! Glad to see you! Hey, Baron Yandel’s son, Bjorn!”

...He probably didn’t recognize me?

As the saying goes, a thief fears a bleeding nose.

Thump thump thump thump—

Though I thought I was unrecognizable, my heart started pounding wildly.

“Baron Yandel’s son? That’s an unusual introduction.”

Okay, it seemed he hadn’t suspected anything yet...

Ha, I stumbled over my words in surprise earlier.

“That kind of remark is something I hear often.”

I answered calmly in a barbarian fashion and diligently summarized the situation.

Jerome Saintred.

More than twenty years ago, he came down to Noirark hunting for fragments of the record stone and fought me.

Apart from the anxiety about whether he recognized me, the situation was nearly the worst.

Because he held the title of baron.

In short, it meant the title blocked any attempt to crush me...

‘I never imagined they’d send such a heavyweight to a place with no known escape method.’

This was behavior unbecoming of the royal family.

So naturally, I expected him to stand aside and watch the situation unfold, but...

“Haha, anyway, it’s so good to meet someone from home in a place like this! Is everything fine in the city?”

“The city? It’s naturally well protected under the royal family’s patronage...”

“Glad to hear it! Well then, we’ll be off. Good luck with the exploration! Take care! What’s everyone doing? Aren’t you preparing to leave?”

After naturally saying goodbye and turning his back, he suddenly appeared in front of me as if by teleportation, blocking my path.

That time I saw him use the grade 3 teleportation skill [Gate of Light].

“Baron, where are you heading?”

“Ah... I’m hungry...”

“You were inconsiderate. Your food might have run out, but don’t worry now. We have enough supplies.”

So, they weren’t going to let me run away?

I dropped the playful tone and lowered my voice.

“Baron Saintred, step aside.”

A cold attitude clearly distinguishing business from personal matters.

But he calmly continued speaking despite my mood.

“That’s impossible. You are the first explorer to enter the unknown floor, and one of our expedition goals is to rescue you.”

“Sounds like someone asked for a rescue.”

“You are a precious ❀ Nоvеlігht ❀ (Don’t copy, read here) treasure of our kingdom. Losing you would be a huge loss.”

“So? What do you plan to do now?”

I asked bluntly.

“Are you planning to forcibly conscript me, a clan leader, into the expedition? By what authority?”

I said mockingly, but he showed no offense and just smiled.

“To be clear, I have no right to forcibly enlist you. Though there is a law to conscript explorers in disasters, it doesn’t apply to nobles like you. However...”

He seemed to expect my resistance and pulled a scroll from his coat.

I knew immediately what it was.

“This is a royal decree.”

In other words, disobeying whatever is written on this paper would be considered treason...

“Baron Bjorn Yandel of Lapdonia, hear this.”

He unfurled the scroll and solemnly read the contents.

“Chaos reigns with evil rebels roaming free. Though we trust the royal light to protect this beautiful city and its people forever, those who blindly believe false rumors and vanish in greed cause great harm to the city.”

The introduction was long, befitting a royal document.

“To summarize, as ruler I cannot stand by, so I have formed this expedition army and sent it forth...”

In short, the core message was:

“Baron Bjorn Yandel is to join the first expedition army, thoroughly explore the unexplored regions under the command of Commander Jerome Saintred, and find a way to return to the city.”

Damn, they really messed me up this time.

The royal decree had been issued.

Officially, the king could hardly be bedridden and issuing orders; this was surely a royal command authorized by the prime minister.

Yet, despite that, I had only one choice.

“...I will obey the royal decree.”

Unless I killed every witness and lied that I never received any royal orders, refusal was impossible.

“The royal family will remember Baron Yandel’s loyalty.”

Jerome’s gentle smile was infuriating, but I decided to focus on the positive side.

Joining the expedition and being under his command?

My freedom on the underground first floor would diminish, and there’d be no way to restrain him if he acted like a troll.

Unlike now, where I monopolize everything, loot would be divided according to fixed shares, and I’d be left to scavenge leftovers.

But...

‘With that force, we can do anything.’

It was basically like having a cheat key in hand.

At least from # Nоvеlight # now on, I wouldn’t face difficulties due to lack of “force” while exploring the underground first floor.

Whether it was the still unfinished Library Island, the Tree Island still half shrouded in mystery, or the Giant Island with colossal beings.

With this force, anything was possible.

In other words, the possibility of escaping this floor and returning to the city had skyrocketed...

“Now, shall we start by organizing the information? Do you have any logs? Explorers apparently keep logs to record their activities.”

Ugh, really trying to take it easy.

In an age where knowledge is wealth, meeting the royal army felt more like encountering a band of thieves.

Therefore...

“Before sharing information, I want to talk about the loot first.”

We decided to settle that issue first.

“Hmm, the loot?”

“Yes. The royal decree ordered you to explore under your command. It didn’t say anything about treatment.”

“That’s true. But don’t worry too much. Our expedition’s goal isn’t some petty money, and the royal family already said to ensure the baron’s convenience.”

“Still, I want to be clear about something.”

“The command over explorers under your command remains yours. Also, the income you earn from exploration is fully guaranteed except in one case.”

Command rights preserved and exploration income untouched.

A relief amidst misfortune, but ominously, there was a condition.

“One exception...?”

“This expedition is for the royal family, and more broadly for the bright future of the nation. Regardless of whether it’s essence, materials, equipment, or special magical items, if it’s something that has never existed before, our expedition will take it for public research.”

In other words, they would seize every first discovery.

Honestly, my first thought was, ‘Where do these bastards come from?’ but Jerome skillfully used the carrot and stick approach.

Bellarios.

A grade 3 monster possessing a skill specialized in magic damage defense—my only real weakness.

Magic resistance +200.

All attribute affinity +30.

Water, fire, wind, earth, and so on. No matter what elemental skill is used, this affinity amplifies its effectiveness.

Stats can always be filled in.

(P) Ring of Circulation — When a grade 3 or higher monster dies within radius, there is a chance to increase stats.

A passive that increases stats.

In some ways, it’s similar to the Manticore’s [Inheritance], which increases stats by consuming magic stones, but there is a crucial difference.

[Ring of Circulation] has no limits.

Well, as stats increase, activation chance decreases, so after a total of 400, it almost doesn’t go up anymore.

Anyway.

(A) Scale of Greed — Adds additional correction proportional to magic resistance.

Ultimately, the core of this essence is its active skill.

‘Deep Sea Giant has 35, Bayon has 20, Ogre 80, Bellarios 200.’

[Ring of Circulation] has an activation mechanism.

If a defeated monster is within 1m radius, the chance of increasing physical stats rises significantly; within 5m radius, it’s completely random.

If more than 10m away, it’s influenced by stats related to supernatural abilities.

Ah, naturally, I researched this myself.

Shreek—

The village chief’s combat power radiated dazzling aura.

Together with Gahwin’s ancient magic, and even Elwen who confused monsters and turned them into minions to aid in battle, the fights were so easy they were almost boring.

Yes, the battles.

[Control is decreasing.]

On the journey along the canyon path into the abyssal underground, there was no conversation at all.

This rule had been applied since yesterday.

All chatter except for strategy discussions was completely prohibited.

“...”

“...”

There’s a saying that words are seeds.

In other words, if no words are spoken, no conflict will arise...

“...How long will you keep up this ridiculous behavior?”

As in all things, there were rebels who were dissatisfied with the rules even here.

Well, it wasn’t a big problem.

“Baron! I don’t think this helps at all—”

“Shh.”

The wizard frowned and raised the hammer, and suddenly regained composure.

“S-Sorry. I got excited again because of this damned curse... I’m really sorry. This won’t happen again—”

“This is your final warning.”

“...Understood. I’ll be careful.”

Maybe it was because I really hit him once yesterday?

My eyes started to sharpen faster.

Still, aggressive words came from my mouth first.

“Don’t just be careful, do well. It’s just keeping quiet. Is that so hard?”

“Um... Sir...?”

“...I got a bit excited too. From now on, I’ll keep my mouth shut.”

Phew... that’s also why chatter is banned.

In such an impulsive and sensitive situation, no stimulus at all is the best.

‘I’m going crazy.’

It’s so hard I feel like losing my mind.

If I lose focus for a moment, aggression surfaces.

I wonder how the chief and archbishop can stay so normal.

Honestly, the chief is understandable, but the archbishop didn’t look mentally that extraordinary.

“Sir! That’s a flower over there!”

“Wasn’t it my turn this time?”

The moment Elwen pointed somewhere, I hurried over, picked a flower growing on the cliff edge, and put it in my mouth.

Simple reason.

[Control slightly increases.]

This flower alleviates mental abnormal symptoms when ingested.

By the way, I was the one who discovered it.

I had fed it to Gahwin because it looked suspicious whenever I saw it.

“...Sir, you must be really struggling, right?”

“...I appreciate the thought, but please try not to talk to me if possible. It doesn’t sound good in my current state.”

“Ah... I’m sorry.”

Now that I think about it, Elwen is quite something.

Before she lost her essence, every moment must have been similar to my current state—

“Uh? Everyone stop for a moment!”

Then Gahwin shouted loudly and stopped us.

“Did you hear something below? Something down there?”

“Am I starting to hear hallucinations now...”

I sighed and lightly raised ✧ NоvеIight ✧ (Original source) the hammer, but the chief stopped me.

“Baron, put the hammer down for now. Lord Versilus’s words are true.”

What? There really was a sound?

I gestured to Elwen to confirm, but she just shook her head.

It seems the stat reductions caused her not to hear anything...

“Focus. Then you should be able to hear it too, Baron.”

Closing my eyes and focusing on the sound, I faintly heard something through the breeze brushing past my ears.

[This way...]

Something was whispering, calling me.

“Finally, something has come out.”

This might be chilling for someone, but to me, it was very welcome.

After all, how many days had I been suffering?

I just wanted to clear this chapter and leave here as soon as possible.

“What will you do?”

“What else but check what it is?”

This time Gahwin didn’t oppose my words.

Well, I guess the old man wanted this to end quickly as well.

“Follow me closely.”

After increasing our speed, the noise coming from below became clearer.

[Good...]

[Come this way...]

[You have a very beautiful soul.]

Even though I didn’t like having to move as if entranced while hearing such blatant lines, there was nothing I could do.

Whether it was a mid-boss or the real boss, I had to capture it first for something to happen—

“By the way, Baron...”

Someone spoke to me then.

“Have you ever thought of serving something other than your ancestors’ spirits?”

Surprisingly, it wasn’t Gahwin, but the archbishop who said this.

Somehow I chuckled.

“Heh heh...”

I thought he was holding up well, but maybe this old man was reaching his limit too?

“Trying to proselytize to barbarians is extremely rude, you know?”

“...”

“But given the situation, no need to make excuses. I’m not that angry.”

Since he was a model elder who never caused trouble, I tried to pass it off coolly.

But despite my efforts, the archbishop just looked at me with an unpleasant smile.

Maybe because of that?

“...?”

I noticed the change late.

‘The voice...’

Words wouldn’t come out.

It felt as if something blocked my throat.

When I quickly checked around, I soon realized the problem wasn’t only mine.

“...”

“...”

The surroundings were too quiet.

Not just because of the chatter ban.

No breath, no footsteps.

Not even the sound of the wind blowing from below during the journey.

[This way...]

Except for the mysterious voice, there was perfect silence.

The moment I realized this precisely.

Thump—! Thump—! Thump—!

A short murmur and vibrations from my toes.

The vibrations from below the cliff were growing stronger.

“...!”

“...!”

Looking around, everyone had stopped with their mouths slightly open.

They seemed to be saying something, but it was hard to understand.

I cautiously approached the cliff edge and looked down.

Thump-!

Darkness without end.

Nothing was visible yet.

But...

Thump-!

Soon, ‘it’ appeared from the darkness.

It looked up at me with chilling eyes and was crawling up the cliff.

‘Uh...’

The moment I met its gaze, my body froze like stone.

I couldn’t understand it at all.

Thump-!

Before I entered here.

Thump-!

The wizards who had thoroughly investigated the portal insisted that beyond it was definitely a fissure...
